# Leads verification report (post-tour)

Approved by the operator on 2026-11-04 ("approved: leads verification"). Read-only: nothing was changed, deleted, run or tested. This report is an input to the post-tour analysis, which the direction session leads; it decides nothing.

**Sealed for the operator until their tour remarks are done** (the tour keeps the inspector's notes from the operator until the post-tour analysis; itinerary :74).

## Method

- Source: every item of the inspector's section "(2) Legacy or simplifiable" in stops 00 to 29, plus the 7 leads parked in `00_itinerary.md` ("Leads found while mapping"), IDs P-1 to P-7.
- Pass 1 (Sonnet, 6 agents, brief `BRIEF.md`): each lead verified by search; verdict CONFIRMED / PARTLY / REFUTED / NOT CHECKABLE HERE / DESIGN CALL, with evidence and a removal footprint.
- Pass 2 (4 independent agents, brief `PASS2_BRIEF.md`): every CONFIRMED or PARTLY lead re-verified from scratch, trying to refute it; verdict UPHELD / NARROWED / OVERTURNED / FOOTPRINT CORRECTED.
- Final status: pass 1's verdict, turned to REFUTED if pass 2 overturned it and to PARTLY if pass 2 narrowed it. FOOTPRINT CORRECTED keeps the verdict; read pass 2's column for the fuller footprint.
- Flag set judged under: the tour's (all `orchestrator:` flags on except `deviation_gate`, `zero_trade_windows_not_computed`, `extra_cards_at_1a`), not this checkout's committed config.
- Checkout: master 78790e5f (2026-10-28). Not available here: SR/runs/ after run_060 (so run_102), the live queue and campaign records after run_097. Leads needing them are NOT CHECKABLE HERE or carry that note in their row.
- Inspector items that are its own "not checked" lists: 22a-6 has a NOT CHECKABLE HERE row; 25-7 has no row.
- Raw files: `batch_01.md` to `batch_06.md` (pass 1, each with a "Not checked" list), `pass2_*.md` (pass 2).
